# UI Owned Test Axis Setup Preservation

Actual full source discovery admitted8701 Rust test paths and found207 affected files /343 exact axis/parser sites. The initial exploratory rg subprocess hit its default stdout buffer and exposed only a partial33-file/51-site roster; this was detected before any source writes, never admitted as full coverage. The corrected subprocess checks its exit status and explicitly provides enough buffer for the full physical roster. The actual old Default bases/axis helpers are source RED against the current explicit constructor and strict language schema contracts.

Original full bodies were captured durably before editing. Every nonaxis named field, assertion, fixture and feature setting must remain unchanged outside the exact required constructor/helper edits. UI High owns direct-manifest graph participation. No compiler or runtime success is claimed.
